# Card 065 proof: both players take a Morita, walk to the shooting range and shoot all its targets. The client
# kills the first three (C_1, C_2, C_11), the host the rest: six more targets, then the bug nest with a grenade.
# Waves pop as players walk down the range (the targets' Activate runs on the host only; card 065 relays it).
# Damage stays on the host: each kill is a host
# IncrementObjectiveCount(ShootingRange_SO3), relayed by card 049. No teleport, no xdotool.
# Run: SCENARIO=tools/scenarios/c65-range.txt STAGGER=5 HOST_EXTRA_ENV="UBWCOOP_AUTODROPSHIP=1" tools/run_pair.sh <tag> 600
# A target bobs (root z -336 to 234, about 6 s); it can be hit only near the top. Each cycle re-aims 60 uu above
# its root and fires 250 ms (after 0.15 s, so the aim reaches the host) only while the look line puts that point
# at z 130 or higher (the target is up). `unless` stops the cycles once the kill count is reached. Both rules
# keep the client inside its 60 rounds: its reserve shows 0 and it cannot reload (see the card 065 report).

host wait state pawn=host-own timeout=240
client wait state pawn=client-own timeout=120
host ui continue
sleep 3
host goto actor BP_WeaponPickup_MoritaMk1_C 120
host look actor BP_WeaponPickup_MoritaMk1_C
host key E
host wait log "card021: host GiveWeaponByClass\(BP_WeaponDefinition_MoritaMk1_C.*host-own" timeout=15
client goto actor BP_WeaponPickup_MoritaMk1_C 120
client look actor BP_WeaponPickup_MoritaMk1_C
client key E
host wait log "card021: host GiveWeaponByClass\(BP_WeaponDefinition_MoritaMk1_C.*server-copy" timeout=15
client key Two
client wait log "switch to weapon 1: Equip sent" timeout=15
host key Two
host goto 4580 13300 105 300
client goto 5300 13300 105 400
host shot at-range
client shot at-range
client wait log "card065: client .*TargetRangeTarger_C_1[ .]ADLevelTriggerReactions" timeout=30
host look actor BP_KillableBuilding_Base_TargetRangeTarger_C_1 60
client look actor BP_KillableBuilding_Base_TargetRangeTarger_C_1 60
sleep 1
host shot wave1
client shot wave1
# kill 1: client shoots C_1
unless host log "card049: host sent IncrementObjectiveCount\(ShootingRange_SO3\)" count=1 client look actor BP_KillableBuilding_Base_TargetRangeTarger_C_1 60
unless host log "card049: host sent IncrementObjectiveCount\(ShootingRange_SO3\)" count=1 when client last "cmd: [0-9]+ look: " " at -?[0-9]+ -?[0-9]+ (1[3-9][0-9]|2[0-9][0-9]) from " sleep 0.15
